## `smartjudge_module.py`

### 1. Add PII mask/unmask utilities (missing — new code)
Insert this new block right after the `_ASCII_TO_FA = str.maketrans(...)` line (before `_normalize_fa`):

```python
_PII_PATTERNS = [
    ("IBAN", r'\bIR\d{24}\b'),
    ("MOBILE", r'(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)'),
    ("NID", r'(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)'),
]


def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
    """ماسک کردن کد ملی، موبایل و شبا پیش از ارسال متن کاربر به LLM شخص ثالث."""
    if not text:
        return text or "", {}
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
    counters: Dict[str, int] = {}
    for label, pattern in _PII_PATTERNS:
        def _sub(m, label=label):
            counters[label] = counters.get(label, 0) + 1
            token = f"⟦PII_{label}_{counters[label]}⟧"
            mapping[token] = m.group(0)
            return token
        text = re.sub(pattern, _sub, text)
    return text, mapping


def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """بازگرداندن مقادیر واقعی PII در خروجی LLM."""
    if not text or not mapping:
        return text
    for token, original in mapping.items():
        text = text.replace(token, original)
    return text
```

Wire it in three places:

**a) `_llm_analyze`** — right after `is_informational = (query_type == QUERY_RESEARCH)`, add:

Use `_retrial_build_user_prompt(masked_case, masked_verdict)` in the `_chat_completion` call, and right after it returns add `content = unmask_pii_entities(content, pii_map)`.

**c) `synthesize_case` endpoint** — before building `system_prompt`/`ctx`, add:

    (re.compile(r'\b[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w+\b'), '<EMAIL>'),
    (re.compile(r'\b\d{10}\b'), '<NATIONAL_ID>'),  # example

]

    masked_text = text

            if original not in mapping:

```python
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

# Model tiers - exactly 2 models per tier as required
TIER_MODELS: Dict[str, List[str]] = {
    "tier1": ["google/gemini-3.5-flash-lite", "deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash-0731"],
    "tier2": ["openai/gpt-5.6-luna", "deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro"],
    "tier3": ["openai/gpt-5.6-luna", "deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro"],
}

# PII patterns for masking (phone, email, national ID, etc.)
_PII_PATTERNS = [
    (re.compile(r'\b09\d{9}\b'), '<PHONE>'),          # Iranian mobile numbers
    (re.compile(r'\b\d{10}\b'), '<NATIONAL_ID>'),     # Iranian national ID
    (re.compile(r'\b[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w+\b'), '<EMAIL>'),
    (re.compile(r'\b\d{3}-\d{3}-\d{4}\b'), '<PHONE>'), # US-style phone
]

def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
    """
    Replace PII in the input text with placeholders.
    Returns the masked text and a mapping of original -> placeholder.
    """
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
    masked_text = text

    for pattern, placeholder in _PII_PATTERNS:
        def repl(match, ph=placeholder):
            original = match.group(0)
            if original not in mapping:
                mapping[original] = ph
            return ph
        masked_text = pattern.sub(repl, masked_text)

    return masked_text, mapping

def unmask_pii_entities(masked_text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """
    Restore original PII values from placeholders using the mapping.
    """
    for original, placeholder in mapping.items():
        masked_text = masked_text.replace(placeholder, original)
    return masked_text

def _llm_analyze(case_text: str, model: str) -> str:
    """
    Analyze a case using an LLM, masking PII before the call and unmasking after.
    """
    masked_text, mapping = mask_pii_entities(case_text)
    # Simulated LLM call - replace with actual API call
    result = f"Analysis of {masked_text} using {model}"
    return unmask_pii_entities(result, mapping)

def _retrial_evaluate_engine(case_text: str, model: str) -> str:
    """
    Evaluate a retrial using an LLM, masking PII before the call and unmasking after.
    """
    masked_text, mapping = mask_pii_entities(case_text)
    # Simulated LLM call - replace with actual API call
    result = f"Retrial evaluation of {masked_text} using {model}"
    return unmask_pii_entities(result, mapping)

def synthesize_case(case_text: str, model: str) -> str:
    """
    Synthesize a case using an LLM, masking PII before the call and unmasking after.
    """
    masked_text, mapping = mask_pii_entities(case_text)
    # Simulated LLM call - replace with actual API call
    result = f"Synthesized case from {masked_text} using {model}"
    return unmask_pii_entities(result, mapping)
```

Please note that the simulated LLM calls are placeholders; you will need to replace them with your actual API integration. The PII patterns are examples and should be adjusted to match your specific data.

## `output_apps/generated_modules/smartjudge/smartjudge_module.py`

### 1. Add PII masking utilities
Add near the top, after the `_FA_TO_ASCII`/`_ASCII_TO_FA` translation tables:

```python
_PII_IBAN_RE = re.compile(r'\bIR\d{24}\b', re.IGNORECASE)
_PII_PHONE_RE = re.compile(r'(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)')
_PII_NATIONAL_ID_RE = re.compile(r'(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)')


def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
    """پوشش داده‌های هویتی حساس (شماره شبا، موبایل ۰۹xx، کدملی ۱۰ رقمی) پیش از ارسال به LLM خارجی.
    ترتیب پردازش: IBAN → موبایل → کدملی (طولانی‌ترین الگو ابتدا تا از تطابق جزئی جلوگیری شود)."""
    if not text:
        return text or "", {}
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
    counters = {"IBAN": 0, "PHONE": 0, "NID": 0}

    def _mask(pattern: "re.Pattern", label: str, s: str) -> str:
        def _sub(m: "re.Match") -> str:
            counters[label] += 1
            placeholder = f"[[{label}_{counters[label]}]]"
            mapping[placeholder] = m.group(0)
            return placeholder
        return pattern.sub(_sub, s)

    masked = _mask(_PII_IBAN_RE, "IBAN", text)
    masked = _mask(_PII_PHONE_RE, "PHONE", masked)
    masked = _mask(_PII_NATIONAL_ID_RE, "NID", masked)
    return masked, mapping


def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """بازگرداندن داده‌های هویتی اصلی در متن خروجی LLM."""
    if not text or not mapping:
        return text or ""
    restored = text
    for placeholder, original in mapping.items():
        restored = restored.replace(placeholder, original)
    return restored
```

### 2. Wire masking into `_llm_analyze`
- Keep `rag, graph = await asyncio.gather(_rag_retrieve(case_text), _graph_retrieve(case_text))` using the **original unmasked** `case_text` (internal retrieval only).
- Before building `user_prompt`, mask the text:
  ```python
  masked_case_text, pii_map = mask_pii_entities(case_text)
  masked_files = [mask_pii_entities(f)[0] for f in attached_files] if attached_files else attached_files
  user_prompt = _user_prompt(masked_case_text, masked_files)
  ```
- Immediately after `content, model_used = await _chat_completion(...)` (the main call), add:
  ```python
  content = unmask_pii_entities(content, pii_map)
  ```
- In the Task 6 draft-only fallback block, replace `_user_prompt(case_text, attached_files)` with `_user_prompt(masked_case_text, masked_files)`, and unmask `draft_content` the same way right after that `_chat_completion` call.

  pii_map.update(verdict_map)

Replace the current `TIER_MODELS` dict with:

### 1. Add boilerplate guard for badges in `renderCaseResult`
Add a small helper near `escapeHtml`:
```js
const BOILERPLATE_TOKENS = ['نامشخص', 'نیاز به', 'ارائه نشده', 'قابل ارائه نیست', 'مشخص نیست'];
function isBoilerplateText(s) {
    if (!s) return true;
    return BOILERPLATE_TOKENS.some(tok => s.includes(tok));
}
```
In `renderCaseResult`, change the win/jurisdiction badge conditions from `if (win)` / `if (jur)` to:
```js
if (winEl) {
    if (win && !isBoilerplateText(win)) { winEl.innerText = '🎯 شانس موفقیت: ' + win; winEl.classList.remove('hidden'); }
    else { winEl.innerText = ''; winEl.classList.add('hidden'); }
}
if (jurEl) {
    if (jur && !isBoilerplateText(jur)) { jurEl.innerText = '🏛️ مرجع صالح: ' + jur; jurEl.classList.remove('hidden'); }
    else { jurEl.innerText = ''; jurEl.classList.add('hidden'); }
}
```

### Diff 1 — Add PII masking utilities

```diff
 _FA_MAP = str.maketrans({"ي": "ی", "ك": "ک", "ة": "ه", "\u200c": " ", "أ": "ا", "إ": "ا", "ؤ": "و", "ئ": "ی"})
 _FA_TO_ASCII = str.maketrans("۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹", "0123456789")
 _ASCII_TO_FA = str.maketrans("0123456789", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹")
 
 
+# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+# Task 5 — پوشش داده‌های هویتی حساس (PII Masking) پیش از ارسال به LLM خارجی
+# کدملی ۱۰ رقمی، شماره موبایل ۰۹xx و شماره شبا (IBAN) پیش از خروج از سرور با
+# نگهدارنده (placeholder) جایگزین می‌شوند و پس از دریافت پاسخ LLM بازگردانده می‌شوند.
+# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+_PII_IBAN_RE = re.compile(r'\bIR\d{24}\b', re.IGNORECASE)
+_PII_PHONE_RE = re.compile(r'(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)')
+_PII_NATIONAL_ID_RE = re.compile(r'(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)')
+
+
+def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
+    """پوشش شماره شبا، موبایل ۰۹xx و کدملی ۱۰ رقمی — ترتیب IBAN → موبایل → کدملی
+    (طولانی‌ترین الگو ابتدا تا از تطابق جزئی جلوگیری شود). خروجی: (متن پوشش‌داده‌شده, نگاشت بازگشت)."""
+    if not text:
+        return text or "", {}
+    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
+    counters = {"IBAN": 0, "PHONE": 0, "NID": 0}
+
+    def _mask(pattern: "re.Pattern", label: str, s: str) -> str:
+        def _sub(m: "re.Match") -> str:
+            counters[label] += 1
+            placeholder = f"[[{label}_{counters[label]}]]"
+            mapping[placeholder] = m.group(0)
+            return placeholder
+        return pattern.sub(_sub, s)
+
+    masked = _mask(_PII_IBAN_RE, "IBAN", text)
+    masked = _mask(_PII_PHONE_RE, "PHONE", masked)
+    masked = _mask(_PII_NATIONAL_ID_RE, "NID", masked)
+    return masked, mapping
+
+
+def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
+    """بازگرداندن داده‌های هویتی اصلی در متن خروجی LLM."""
+    if not text or not mapping:
+        return text or ""
+    restored = text
+    for placeholder, original in mapping.items():
+        restored = restored.replace(placeholder, original)
+    return restored
+
+
 def _normalize_fa(text: str) -> str:
     return (text or "").translate(_FA_MAP).lower()
```
